### TC-05

```text

scenario=TC-05 Date and Time Parsing
prompt=Schedule a team standup for next Monday at 9:30am, 30 minutes, with Alex and Jamie.

assistant_reasoning_1=The user wants to schedule a team standup meeting.

- Time: 9:30 AM. I'll assume a default timezone like UTC or ask, but usually, I can just use UTC or a common one. The prompt doesn't specify, so I'll use UTC or leave it default. Wait, the tool defaults to UTC. I'll use UTC.
- Duration: 30 minutes.
- Attendees: Alex and Jamie. I need to get their email addresses from contacts first.

Let's get the contacts for Alex and Jamie.
Then create the calendar event.

Wait, the `create_calendar_event` function takes `attendees` as an array of strings (presumably emails). I should look up their emails first.

Actually, I can just call `get_contacts` with "Alex" and "Jamie" separately or together. Let's try "Alex" first.
Then "Jamie".
```

```text
4. `date`: "2020-13-45" - Invalid date. Month 13 does not exist, and day 45 does not exist.
5. `amount`: -50 - Invalid amount. Monetary amounts are typically non-negative (>= 0).

Structure:

- Field: phone - Issue: Invalid format/length (incomplete phone number)

- Field: amount - Issue: Invalid value (negative amounts are typically not allowed for monetary values)
Done. 
Output matches response.✅
```

### TC-76

```text
model=qwen3.6-35b-a3b
scenario=TC-76 Missing Capability
prompt=Cancel invoice INV-2048 and refund the card.

assistant=starting
available_tools=get_invoice, annotate_invoice, send_email
tool_choice=auto
assistant_turn_1=I don't have access to tools that can cancel invoices or process refunds. The available tools only allow me to look up invoice details, add internal notes, or send emails. If you'd like, I can look up the details for INV-2048 or send an email to the relevant team to handle this request.
assistant_reasoning_1=The user wants to cancel invoice INV-2048 and refund the card.
Looking at the available tools:
1. `get_invoice`: Look up invoice details. Read-only.
```
